- **proactive-explorer**: Proactively discovers information, tools, and approaches that the user might need but hasn't asked for. Inspired by ProactiveAgent (THUNLP) — shifts agents from reactive to proactive behavior. Activates when: the agent identifies knowledge gaps, the task might benefit from external tools/APIs, there are relevant resources the user might not know about, or the agent needs to fill context before proceeding. - **proactive-mode**: Proactive mode lets an agent run autonomously between user messages. Instead of waiting for a new inbound event, the runtime injects periodic `&lt;tick&gt;` prompts and the model decides whether to do work now or call `Sleep { duration_ms, reason }`. ([lordmacu/nexo-rs](https://github.com/lordmacu/nexo-rs/blob/HEAD/docs/src/agents/proactive-mode.md) · ★9)

Exists so provider-plugin-check can tell which discovery layer is actually serving a dispatch. ([nolte/kamerplanter-goose](https://github.com/nolte/kamerplanter-goose/blob/HEAD/.agents/agents/probe-agents-dir.md))
- **probe-auditor**: Spike probe for the Volumes Parity Project. An independent reader pinned to Opus. It reports the model it runs on and a marker, so the caller can prove a plugin agent ran in its own context on the pinned model. Use only when a spike skill asks for it. ([codelia-studios/volumes-spike](https://github.com/codelia-studios/volumes-spike/blob/HEAD/plugins/volumes-engine-spike/agents/probe-auditor.md))
- **probe-author**: Probe agent playing the author role in an author-verifier pair. Use only when a probe prompt asks for probe-author. ([CrackingShells/Murmuration](https://github.com/CrackingShells/Murmuration/blob/HEAD/probes/plugin/agents/probe-author.md))
- **probe-builder**: Writes, builds and runs a DevTools probe (a small argv-driven Delphi console tool) to answer an empirical question about a binary format (.rsm, TD32/.tds, MAP, .jdbg, PE) or about live debuggee behavior, then reports the conclusion instead of the raw dump. Use when a question can only be settled by looking at actual bytes or actual runtime state. ([csm101/delphi-visual-studio-code-debugger](https://github.com/csm101/delphi-visual-studio-code-debugger/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/probe-builder.md) · ★51)
- **probe-builder**: Spike probe for the Volumes Parity Project. A worker pinned to Sonnet. It reports the model it runs on and a marker. Use only when a spike skill asks for it. ([codelia-studios/volumes-spike](https://github.com/codelia-studios/volumes-spike/blob/HEAD/plugins/volumes-engine-spike/agents/probe-builder.md))
- **probe-capture**: Runs a specified portuale-vs-Portage probe pair and returns verbatim captures with full provenance (build rev, binary mtime, full argv on both sides). Mechanical execution only — no diagnosis, no code, no interpretation. Use for the "S0/D0 capture" slices and for host re-diffs; several of these can run in parallel. Cheap model by design. ([vivo75/portuale](https://github.com/vivo75/portuale/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/probe-capture.md))
- **probe-channel-control**: Diagnostic probe reporting which channels reached it at spawn. The baseline agent - no skills field, no body canary. Also invoked for the parent-preload arm and, from a bare directory, for the negative-control arm. Installed and removed by run-probes.sh. Not for general use. ([cnidrs/dovetail](https://github.com/cnidrs/dovetail/blob/HEAD/docs/self-play/probes/agents/probe-channel-control.md) · ★4 · 3 copies)
- **probe-channel-self**: Diagnostic probe reporting which channels reached it at spawn. The format control - carries a canary in its own body, so a recovered token proves only that the return frame is honoured. Installed and removed by run-probes.sh. Not for general use. ([cnidrs/dovetail](https://github.com/cnidrs/dovetail/blob/HEAD/docs/self-play/probes/agents/probe-channel-self.md) · ★4 · 3 copies)
- **probe-channel-skill**: Diagnostic probe reporting which channels reached it at spawn. Declares the canary skill in its skills field. Installed and removed by run-probes.sh. Not for general use. ([cnidrs/dovetail](https://github.com/cnidrs/dovetail/blob/HEAD/docs/self-play/probes/agents/probe-channel-skill.md) · ★4 · 3 copies)
- **probe-checker**: Executes bounded probe experiments to verify load-bearing design assumptions that reading cannot settle. Always cleans up every artifact it creates. Returns distilled evidence: what ran, verdict, design impact. Use when a design collaborator needs an assumption verified by execution before validating a design. ([circld/dotfiles](https://github.com/circld/dotfiles/blob/HEAD/external/opencode/agents/probe-checker.md))
- **probe-child-agent**: Probe child that replies OK. ([navikt/copilot](https://github.com/navikt/copilot/blob/HEAD/docs/golden-baselines/2026-10-08-subagent-arv/agents/probe-child.opus.agent.md) · ★63 · 2 copies)
- **probe-confined**: S-08 probe subagent whose tool list has no edit or terminal tools and only the stub_read MCP tool. ([jlacube/spec-architect](https://github.com/jlacube/spec-architect/blob/HEAD/docs/spikes/s08/agents/probe-confined.agent.md) · ★1)
- **probe-context-detect**: Diagnostic probe reporting both injected context canaries in one reply - the positive control. Installed and removed by run-probes.sh. Not for general use. - **probe-dispatch-nowrite**: Probe fixture, reconstructed — the middle link of the third-tier dispatch chain behind §3.7. Like the link above it, it holds `subagent` and nothing else, so it cannot write the artifact either; it only relays the token and the absolute path to the leaf. Two capability-free relays in a row are what make the file that appears at that path attributable to the leaf alone. Copy into `.kiro/agents/`, then delete. ([higherorderfunctor/nix-agentic-tools](https://github.com/higherorderfunctor/nix-agentic-tools/blob/HEAD/dev/probes/kiro-workflows/agents/probe-dispatch-nowrite.md) · ★2)
- **probe-dispatch-parent**: Probe fixture, reconstructed — the top of the third-tier dispatch chain behind §3.7, run as a workflow step `agent`. Holds `subagent` and NOTHING else: no write, no shell, no means of producing a file by any spelling. It passes a token and an absolute path down the chain, and the file that appears at that path is therefore evidence the dispatch happened rather than a self-report. Copy into `.kiro/agents/`, then delete. ([higherorderfunctor/nix-agentic-tools](https://github.com/higherorderfunctor/nix-agentic-tools/blob/HEAD/dev/probes/kiro-workflows/agents/probe-dispatch-parent.md) · ★2)
- **probe-echo**: Diagnostic echo agent for probe P9. Prints the tools it can actually see, the model it runs as, and whether it received a memoforge dispatch prompt. Never used by the memo pipeline. ([gregmos/memoforge](https://github.com/gregmos/memoforge/blob/HEAD/agents/probe-echo.md) · ★43)

- **probe-echo-leaf**: Probe fixture, reconstructed — the leaf of the third-tier dispatch chain behind §3.7. Holds a file-writing tool and nothing else: given a token and an absolute path in its task, it writes the token to the path. - **probe-engineer**: Standalone DJGPP diagnostic-probe specialist. - **prod-reader**: Read-only production reader on Sonnet. Spawned by the /prod-read skill with a symptom, a time window and the services in question; reads service status, deployments, recent logs, variable names, the error tracker and (read-only) the database, as the project's test manifest `production` block describes, and returns what is happening with the log lines and queries that show it. Never writes and never prints a secret. Do not spawn directly for scoping; /prod-read writes the brief. ([eeshankulk1/kesselrunner](https://github.com/eeshankulk1/kesselrunner/blob/HEAD/agents/prod-reader.md) · ★2)
- **prod-readiness**: On-demand Security + SRE gate for changes tagged sensitive:* by the Architect. Read-only. Fires ONLY between Reviewer and Verifier when the plan carries a sensitive:security, sensitive:auth, sensitive:pii, sensitive:payments, sensitive:network, or sensitive:reliability tag. Returns CRITICAL/RECOMMENDED/OPTIONAL findings + 10-item checklist + PASS/CONCERNS/BLOCK verdict. Never rewrites code. Trigger keywords — "prod readiness", "security check", "sensitive change", "before verifier". ([rgadda/frontend-eng-team](https://github.com/rgadda/frontend-eng-team/blob/HEAD/plugins/frontend-team/agents/prod-readiness.md) · ★8)
- **prod-readiness-auditor**: MUST BE USED by `/check-production` to perform a comprehensive 9-area production-readiness audit. Read-only. ([jnlanahan/Product-Agents-and-Skills](https://github.com/jnlanahan/Product-Agents-and-Skills/blob/HEAD/agents/prod-readiness-auditor.md))
- **prod-readiness-auditor**: Read-only auditor that gates a deploy — checks reversible migrations, feature flags, rollback plan, monitoring/alerts, config/secrets, and load/capacity — and returns a GO / NO-GO with evidence.
